#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""Generate k82.json from spec/ref-0.9.json with no interpreter imports. Run with python3 -I; --check compares bytes.

Independent of every expander: it sweeps the catalog for its named interfaces and
invoke edges, and expands the adapter edges itself to digests a consumer must match.
"""
import hashlib, json, pathlib, re, sys

HERE = pathlib.Path(__file__).resolve().parent
ROOT = HERE.parents[2]
PIN = "5de4e4995350313e50217880ba1b95de39cd043283b726337d4894efb005d197"
raw = (ROOT / "spec/ref-0.9.json").read_bytes()
envelope = b"isa=0.9\nexpansion=ref\nversion=0.9\n" + raw + b"\n"
digest = hashlib.sha256(envelope).hexdigest()
assert digest == PIN, digest
RECIPES = {r["kind"]: r for r in json.loads(raw)["recipes"]}
assert len(RECIPES) == 219
OPEN = sorted(k for k, r in RECIPES.items() if all(isinstance(p, str) for p in r["parameters"]))
SUB = re.compile(r"^([^\[]+)\[(.*)\]$", re.S)
OPS = {"+": lambda a, b: a + b, "-": lambda a, b: a - b, "*": lambda a, b: a * b,
       "div": lambda a, b: a // b, "mod": lambda a, b: a % b, "bit": lambda a, b: (a >> b) & 1}


def scalar(x, ints):
    if isinstance(x, int):
        return x
    if isinstance(x, str):
        return ints[x]
    return OPS[x[0]](*[scalar(y, ints) for y in x[1:]])


def split(w):
    m = SUB.match(w)
    return (m.group(1), json.loads(m.group(2))) if m else (w, None)


def interface(kind):
    """Names used before any binding in body order; open recipes hold only these constructors."""
    used = []

    def go(body, bound):
        for c in body:
            if "gate" in c:
                for w in c["wires"]:
                    n, _ = split(w)
                    if n not in bound and n not in used:
                        used.append(n)
            elif "alloc" in c:
                bound.add(c["alloc"])
            elif "release" in c:
                assert c["release"] in bound, (kind, c)
            elif "for" in c:
                go(c["body"], bound | {c["for"]})
            elif "inverse" in c:
                go(c["body"], bound)
            else:
                raise AssertionError(f"open recipe {kind}: unexpected constructor {sorted(c)}")

    ints = {p for p in RECIPES[kind]["parameters"]}
    go(RECIPES[kind]["body"], set(ints))
    return sorted(used)


def invokes(j):
    if isinstance(j, dict):
        if "invoke" in j:
            yield j
        for v in j.values():
            yield from invokes(v)
    elif isinstance(j, list):
        for v in j:
            yield from invokes(v)


def expand(kind, args):
    """Expand a catalog typed adapter over for/gate/alloc/release/inverse/invoke. Alloc identities are #k in order."""
    out = []
    serial = [0]

    def emit(gate, wires):
        out.append([gate, wires])

    def wire(w, env):
        n, sub = split(w)
        lst = env["wires"][n]
        if sub is None:
            return list(lst)
        i = scalar(sub, env["ints"])
        assert 0 <= i < len(lst), (kind, w, i, len(lst))
        return [lst[i]]

    def declare(body, table):
        for c in body:
            if "alloc" in c:
                table[c["alloc"]] = c
            if "body" in c:
                declare(c["body"], table)

    def acquire(name, width, env):
        ident = serial[0]
        serial[0] += 1
        env["wires"][name] = [f"#{ident}[{i}]" for i in range(width)]
        env["alloc"][name] = f"#{ident}"
        emit("alloc", [f"#{ident}"])

    def walk(body, env, rev):
        """K19 inverse: traverse constructors in reverse, exchanging allocation and release."""
        declare(body, env["decl"])
        for c in reversed(body) if rev else body:
            if "gate" in c:
                assert c["gate"] in ("x", "cx", "ccx", "swap"), c
                emit(c["gate"], [x for w in c["wires"] for x in wire(w, env)])
            elif "alloc" in c or "release" in c:
                name = c.get("alloc", c.get("release"))
                if ("alloc" in c) != rev:
                    acquire(name, scalar(env["decl"][name]["width"], env["ints"]), env)
                else:
                    env["wires"].pop(name)
                    emit("release", [env["alloc"].pop(name)])
            elif "for" in c:
                span = range(scalar(c["from"], env["ints"]), scalar(c["to"], env["ints"]))
                for i in reversed(span) if rev else span:
                    env["ints"][c["for"]] = i
                    walk(c["body"], env, rev)
                env["ints"].pop(c["for"], None)
            elif "inverse" in c:
                walk(c["body"], env, not rev)
            elif "invoke" in c:
                callee = RECIPES[c["invoke"]]
                ps = callee["parameters"]
                assert all(isinstance(p, str) for p in ps), "this expander covers adapter-to-open edges only"
                ints = {p: scalar(a, env["ints"]) for p, a in zip(ps, c["args"], strict=True)}
                # K82: the interface resolves in the invoking scope; nothing else is imported.
                wires = {n: env["wires"][n] for n in interface(c["invoke"])}
                walk(callee["body"], {"ints": ints, "wires": wires, "alloc": {}, "decl": {}}, rev)
            else:
                raise AssertionError(sorted(c))

    r = RECIPES[kind]
    ints, wires = {}, {}
    for p, a in zip(r["parameters"], args, strict=True):
        if p["type"] == "integer":
            ints[p["name"]] = a
        else:
            assert p["type"] == "wire", p
            wires[p["name"]] = [f"{a}[{i}]" for i in range(scalar(p["width"], ints))]
    walk(r["body"], {"ints": ints, "wires": wires, "alloc": {}, "decl": {}}, False)
    return out


def formal_width(p, ints):
    return scalar(p["width"], ints)


def tuples(kind):
    r = RECIPES[kind]
    names = [p["name"] for p in r["parameters"] if p["type"] == "integer"]
    if names == ["N"]:
        return [{"N": n} for n in (1, 2, 3, 4)]
    assert names == ["N", "M"], names
    op = kind.split(".")[-1]
    # §3.3: zext/sext strictly widen, trunc strictly narrows.
    return [{"N": n, "M": m} for n in range(1, 5) for m in range(1, 5) if (m < n if op == "trunc" else n < m)]


edges = []
for caller, r in sorted(RECIPES.items()):
    for site in invokes(r["body"]):
        callee = site["invoke"]
        if callee not in OPEN:
            continue
        assert caller not in OPEN, (caller, callee)
        formals = {p["name"]: p for p in r["parameters"]}
        names = interface(callee)
        for n in names:
            assert formals[n]["type"] == "wire", (caller, n)
        cases = []
        for t in tuples(caller):
            args = [t[p["name"]] if p["type"] == "integer" else f"r_{p['name']}" for p in r["parameters"]]
            leaves = expand(caller, args)
            text = json.dumps(leaves, separators=(",", ":"), ensure_ascii=False).encode()
            cases.append({"args": args,
                          "bundles": {f"r_{p['name']}": formal_width(p, t) for p in r["parameters"] if p["type"] == "wire"},
                          "leaves": len(leaves),
                          "sha256": hashlib.sha256(text).hexdigest()})
        edges.append({"caller": caller, "callee": callee, "interface": names, "instances": cases})
assert len(edges) == 20, len(edges)
invoked = {e["callee"] for e in edges}


def rec(kind, parameters, body):
    return {"kind": kind, "parameters": parameters, "body": body, "scratch": [], "removable": [], "source": "K82 vector"}


def x(w):
    return {"gate": "x", "wires": [w]}


def each_bit(name, n="N"):
    return {"for": "i", "from": 0, "to": n, "body": [x(f'{name}["i"]')]}


I = {"name": "N", "type": "integer"}


def W(name, width):
    return {"name": name, "type": "wire", "width": width}


OPEN_Q = rec("open.q", ["N"], [each_bit("q")])
ANY = {"class": "any", "state": "clean"}
cases = [
    {"id": "named-interface-resolves-at-the-site", "recipes": [OPEN_Q, rec("use", [I, W("q", "N")], [{"invoke": "open.q", "args": ["N"]}])],
     "root": "use", "args": [2, "r"], "bundles": {"r": 2}, "expect": [["x", ["r[0]"]], ["x", ["r[1]"]]]},
    {"id": "typed-recipe-is-closed", "recipes": [rec("closed", [I], [each_bit("q")]), rec("use", [I, W("q", "N")], [{"invoke": "closed", "args": ["N"]}])],
     "root": "use", "args": [2, "r"], "bundles": {"r": 2}, "expect": "reject"},
    {"id": "unbound-at-the-site-rejects", "recipes": [OPEN_Q, rec("use", [I, W("p", "N")], [{"invoke": "open.q", "args": ["N"]}])],
     "root": "use", "args": [2, "r"], "bundles": {"r": 2}, "expect": "reject"},
    {"id": "integer-binding-is-not-a-wire", "recipes": [OPEN_Q, rec("use", [I, {"name": "q", "type": "integer"}], [{"invoke": "open.q", "args": ["N"]}])],

use bennett_isa_conformance::{json::{self, Json}, ref_language::{Expander, Leaf, Registry}};
use std::collections::BTreeMap;
fn sha(s:&str)->String{bennett_isa_conformance::ref_hash::sha256(s.as_bytes())}
fn norm(leaves:&[Leaf])->Vec<(String,Vec<String>)>{
    let mut ids=BTreeMap::new();
    let mut name=|n:&str|->String{ if n.starts_with('@'){let k=ids.len();format!("#{}",*ids.entry(n.to_string()).or_insert(k))}else{n.to_string()}};
    leaves.iter().map(|l|{
        if let Some((n,_,_,_))=&l.allocation{ if l.gate=="alloc"||l.gate=="release"{ return (l.gate.clone(),vec![name(n)]);} }
        (l.gate.clone(),l.wires.iter().map(|(n,i)|format!("{}[{}]",name(n),i)).collect())
    }).collect()
}
fn render(v:&[(String,Vec<String>)])->String{
    let x:Vec<String>=v.iter().map(|(g,w)|{let ws:Vec<String>=w.iter().map(|s|format!("\"{s}\"")).collect();format!("[\"{g}\",[{}]]",ws.join(","))}).collect();
    format!("[{}]",x.join(","))
}
fn main(){
    let t=std::fs::read_to_string(std::env::args().nth(1).unwrap()).unwrap();
    let catalog=std::fs::read_to_string(std::env::args().nth(2).unwrap()).unwrap();
    let v=json::parse(&t).unwrap();
    let (mut ok,mut bad)=(0,0);
    let mut full=Expander::new(&catalog,Registry::new()).unwrap();
    for e in v.get("edges").unwrap().as_arr().unwrap(){
        let caller=e.get("caller").unwrap().as_str().unwrap();
        for c in e.get("instances").unwrap().as_arr().unwrap(){
            let mut bundles:Vec<(String,usize)>=vec![];
            if let Json::Obj(m)=c.get("bundles").unwrap(){for(k,w) in m.iter(){ if let Json::Num(n)=w{bundles.push((k.clone(),*n as usize));}}}
            let b:Vec<(&str,usize)>=bundles.iter().map(|(a,n)|(a.as_str(),*n)).collect();
            let r=full.expand(caller,c.get("args").unwrap().as_arr().unwrap(),&b);
            match r{
                Ok(l)=>{let n=norm(&l);let got=sha(&render(&n));
                    if got==c.get("sha256").unwrap().as_str().unwrap(){ok+=1}else{bad+=1;println!("EDGE DIFF {caller} {} leaves={} want={}",c.get("args").unwrap(),n.len(),c.get("leaves").unwrap());}}
                Err(x)=>{bad+=1;println!("EDGE ERR {caller} {x}");}
            }
        }
    }
    println!("edges: {ok} match, {bad} differ");
    for c in v.get("cases").unwrap().as_arr().unwrap(){
        let id=c.get("id").unwrap().as_str().unwrap();
        let doc=format!(r#"{{"isa":"0.9","name":"ref","version":"0.9","recipes":{}}}"#,c.get("recipes").unwrap());
        let mut bundles:Vec<(String,usize)>=vec![];
        if let Json::Obj(m)=c.get("bundles").unwrap(){for(k,w) in m.iter(){ if let Json::Num(n)=w{bundles.push((k.clone(),*n as usize));}}}
        let b:Vec<(&str,usize)>=bundles.iter().map(|(a,n)|(a.as_str(),*n)).collect();
        let got=Expander::new(&doc,Registry::new()).and_then(|mut e|e.expand(c.get("root").unwrap().as_str().unwrap(),c.get("args").unwrap().as_arr().unwrap(),&b));
        let want=c.get("expect").unwrap();
        let verdict=match(&got,want){
            (Err(x),Json::Str(_))=>format!("PASS rejected: {x}"),
            (Ok(l),Json::Str(_))=>format!("FAIL accepted {}",render(&norm(l))),
            (Err(x),_)=>format!("FAIL rejected: {x}"),
            (Ok(l),w)=>{let g=render(&norm(l));let w=w.to_string().replace(' ',"");if g==w{"PASS".into()}else{format!("FAIL got {g} want {w}")}}
        };
        println!("{id}: {verdict}");
    }
}

    fn bind(&self, r: &Json, args: &[Json], caller: &Context) -> Result<Context, String> {
        self.bind_seeded(r, args, caller, &Env::new())
    }
    fn bind_seeded(
        &self,
        r: &Json,
        args: &[Json],
        caller: &Context,
        seed: &Env,
    ) -> Result<Context, String> {
        let ps = a(f(r, "parameters")?)?;
        if ps.len() != args.len() {
            return Err("parameter arity".into());
        }
        let mut c = Context {
            ints: seed.clone(),
            controls: caller.controls.clone(),
            source_owner:caller.source_owner.clone(),source_point:caller.source_point.clone(),source_edges:caller.source_edges.clone(),source_steps:caller.source_steps.clone(),source_location:caller.source_location.clone(),
            legacy: ps.iter().all(|p| matches!(p, Json::Str(_))),
            ..Context::default()
        };
        if let Some(names) = r.get("scratch") {
            for name in a(names)? {
                c.scratch_names.insert(s(name)?.into());
            }
        }
        validate_formals(ps)?;
        for (p, v) in ps.iter().zip(args) {
            let (name, ty) = if let Json::Str(n) = p {
                (n.as_str(), "integer")
            } else {
                (s(f(p, "name")?)?, s(f(p, "type")?)?)
            };
            match ty {
                "class" => {
                    c.classes.insert(name.into(), class(v, caller)?);
                }
                "allocation" => {
                    c.handles.insert(
                        name.into(),
                        caller
                            .handles
                            .get(s(v)?)
                            .cloned()
                            .ok_or("unbound allocation declaration")?,
                    );
                }
                "region" => {
                    let region = if let Some(n) = v.as_str() {
                        caller.regions.get(n).cloned().ok_or("unbound region")?
                    } else {
                        keys(v, &["address", "cell", "role", "origin", "cells"])?;
                        let mut r = v.clone();
                        if let Json::Obj(m) = &mut r {
                            let ws = wires(f(v, "cells")?, caller)?;
                            // Store canonical explicit wires separately from caller names.
                            m.insert(
                                "cells".into(),
                                Json::Arr(
                                    ws.iter()
                                        .map(|(n, i)| Json::Str(format!("{n}[{i}]")))
                                        .collect(),
                                ),
                            );
                        }
                        r
                    };
                    let aa = scalar(f(&region, "address")?, &Env::new())?;
                    let ww = scalar(f(&region, "cell")?, &Env::new())?;
                    if aa < 0
                        || ww <= 0
                        || aa != scalar(f(p, "address")?, &c.ints)?
                        || ww != scalar(f(p, "cell")?, &c.ints)?
                        || s(f(&region, "role")?)? != s(f(p, "role")?)?
                        || !matches!(s(f(&region, "role")?)?, "in" | "mut")
                        || !["local:", "interface:"].iter().any(|prefix| {
                            s(f(&region, "origin").unwrap()).is_ok_and(|v| {
                                v.strip_prefix(prefix).is_some_and(|name| {
                                    name.as_bytes()
                                        .first()
                                        .is_some_and(|b| b.is_ascii_alphabetic() || *b == b'_')
                                        && name.bytes().all(|b| {
                                            b.is_ascii_alphanumeric() || b == b'_' || b == b'.'
                                        })
                                })
                            })
                        })
                    {
                        return Err("region shape/role/origin mismatch".into());
                    }
                    let ws = if let Some(n) = v.as_str() {
                        named_wires(n, caller)?
                    } else {
                        wires(f(v, "cells")?, caller)?
                    };
                    let count = 1usize
                        .checked_shl(u32::try_from(aa).map_err(|_| "region exponent")?)
                        .ok_or("region too large for checker")?;
                    if count.checked_mul(ww as usize) != Some(ws.len()) {
                        return Err("region width mismatch".into());
                    }
                    c.wires.insert(name.into(), ws);
                    c.regions.insert(name.into(), region);
                }
                "records" => {

                    let records = if let Some(n) = v.as_str() {

                        // Schemas must match as well as runtime widths.

                            != Json::Arr(vec![f(p, "fields")?.clone(), f(p, "order")?.clone()])

//! Typed, capture-free constructor expansion. Symbol bodies come only from an
//! explicitly supplied closure-hash registry, never from interpreter code.
use crate::{
    json::{self, Json},
    ref_catalog::{index, integer, scalar, Env},
};
use std::{
    cell::RefCell,
    collections::{BTreeMap, BTreeSet},
    rc::Rc,
};
/// Source context remains outside the closed catalog and positional arguments.
#[derive(Clone, Debug)]
pub struct SourceSymbol { pub name:String, pub hash:String, pub oracle_invocation:Option<Json>, pub adjoint:bool }
#[derive(Clone, Debug)]
struct SourceCapture { oracle_invocation:Option<Json>, adjoint:bool }
#[derive(Clone, Debug, PartialEq, Eq, PartialOrd, Ord)]
pub struct SourcePoint { pub symbol:String,pub point:String,pub iteration:Vec<usize>,pub instance:Vec<usize> }
impl SourcePoint { pub fn pseudo(symbol:&str,point:&str)->Self {Self{symbol:symbol.into(),point:point.into(),iteration:Vec::new(),instance:Vec::new()}} }
#[derive(Clone, Debug, PartialEq, Eq, PartialOrd, Ord)]
pub struct SourceEdge {pub point:SourcePoint,pub call_index:usize}
#[derive(Clone, Debug, PartialEq, Eq, PartialOrd, Ord)]
pub struct SourceStep {pub invocation:Vec<usize>,pub symbol:String,pub step:usize,pub reverse:bool}
#[derive(Clone, Debug, PartialEq, Eq, PartialOrd, Ord)]
pub struct SourceLocation {pub path:Vec<SourceEdge>,pub terminal:SourcePoint,pub steps:Vec<SourceStep>}
#[derive(Clone, Debug)]
pub struct SourceBody { pub body:Json,pub symbols:Vec<SourceSymbol>,pub symbol:String,pub oracle:bool,pub points:BTreeMap<String,SourcePoint>,pub rows:Vec<SourcePoint> }
#[derive(Clone, Debug)]
struct SourceOwner {symbol:String,oracle:bool,points:BTreeMap<String,SourcePoint>,rows:Vec<SourcePoint>,raw_reverse:bool}
#[derive(Clone, Debug)]
struct StepPlan {leaves:Vec<Leaf>,arguments:BTreeMap<String,Vec<Wire>>}
pub trait SourceFactory: std::fmt::Debug + Send + Sync {
    fn construct(&self, hash:&str, values:&BTreeMap<String,Json>, regions:&BTreeMap<String,Json>, oracle_invocation:Option<&Json>, adjoint:bool)->Result<SourceBody,String>;
}
#[derive(Clone)]
struct Handle {
    declared_scratch: bool,
    scratch: bool,
    metadata: (String, usize, String, String),
    live: bool,
}
pub type Wire = (String, usize);
pub type Registry = BTreeMap<String, Json>;
#[derive(Clone, Debug, PartialEq)]
pub struct Leaf {
    pub invocation: Vec<usize>,
    pub ordinal: usize,
    pub gate: String,
    pub wires: Vec<Wire>,
    pub controls: Vec<Wire>,
    pub angles: Vec<u64>,
    pub allocation: Option<(String, usize, String, String)>,
    pub event: Option<Json>,
    pub scratch: bool,
    pub source:Option<std::sync::Arc<SourceLocation>>,
}
#[derive(Clone, Default)]
struct Context {
    ints: Env,
    source_owner:Option<std::sync::Arc<SourceOwner>>,
    source_point:Option<SourcePoint>,
    source_edges:Vec<SourceEdge>,
    source_steps:Vec<SourceStep>,
    source_location:Option<std::sync::Arc<SourceLocation>>,

    records: BTreeMap<String, Vec<Context>>,
    record_schemas: BTreeMap<String, Json>,
    regions: BTreeMap<String, Json>,
    classes: BTreeMap<String, String>,
    handles: BTreeMap<String, Rc<RefCell<Handle>>>,
    wires: BTreeMap<String, Vec<Wire>>,
    angles: BTreeMap<String, u64>,
    bodies: BTreeMap<String, (Vec<Json>, Box<Context>)>,
    symbols: BTreeMap<String, String>,
    events: BTreeMap<String, Json>,
    images: BTreeMap<String, Vec<u8>>,
    templates: BTreeMap<String, (Vec<Json>, Box<Context>)>,
    arguments: BTreeMap<String, (String, Vec<Json>, Box<Context>, Option<SourceCapture>)>,
    sources:BTreeMap<String,SourceCapture>,
    invocation_source:Option<SourceCapture>,
    values: BTreeMap<String, (Json, Json)>,
    controls: Vec<Wire>,
    legacy: bool,
    scratch_names: BTreeSet<String>,
    declarations: BTreeMap<String, Json>,
    allocations: BTreeMap<String, (String, usize, String, String)>,
}
impl Context {
    fn location(&mut self)->Option<std::sync::Arc<SourceLocation>> {
        if self.source_location.is_none() {self.source_location=self.source_point.as_ref().map(|point|std::sync::Arc::new(SourceLocation{path:self.source_edges.clone(),terminal:point.clone(),steps:self.source_steps.clone()}));}
        self.source_location.clone()
    }
    fn has_name(&self, n: &str) -> bool {
        self.ints.contains_key(n)
            || self.wires.contains_key(n)
            || self.handles.contains_key(n)
            || self.classes.contains_key(n)
            || self.regions.contains_key(n)
            || self.angles.contains_key(n)
            || self.bodies.contains_key(n)
            || self.symbols.contains_key(n)
            || self.events.contains_key(n)
            || self.images.contains_key(n)
            || self.templates.contains_key(n)
            || self.arguments.contains_key(n)
            || self.values.contains_key(n)
            || self.records.contains_key(n)
    }
    fn clear_name(&mut self, n: &str) {
        self.ints.remove(n);
        self.wires.remove(n);
        self.angles.remove(n);
        self.bodies.remove(n);
        self.symbols.remove(n);
        self.sources.remove(n);
        self.events.remove(n);
        self.images.remove(n);
        self.templates.remove(n);
        self.arguments.remove(n);
        self.values.remove(n);
        self.records.remove(n);
        self.record_schemas.remove(n);
        self.regions.remove(n);
        self.classes.remove(n);
        self.handles.remove(n);
        self.allocations.remove(n);
        self.declarations.remove(n);
        self.scratch_names.remove(n);
    }
    fn record_schema(&self, n: &str) -> Result<Json, String> {
        self.record_schemas
            .get(n)
            .cloned()
            .ok_or("unbound record schema".into())
    }
    fn merge(&mut self, b: &Context) {
        self.ints.extend(b.ints.clone());
        self.wires.extend(b.wires.clone());
        self.angles.extend(b.angles.clone());
        self.bodies.extend(b.bodies.clone());
        self.symbols.extend(b.symbols.clone());
        self.sources.extend(b.sources.clone());
        self.events.extend(b.events.clone());
        self.images.extend(b.images.clone());
        self.templates.extend(b.templates.clone());
        self.arguments.extend(b.arguments.clone());
        self.values.extend(b.values.clone());
        self.records.extend(b.records.clone());
        self.record_schemas.extend(b.record_schemas.clone());
        self.regions.extend(b.regions.clone());
        self.classes.extend(b.classes.clone());
        self.handles.extend(b.handles.clone());
    }
}
fn f<'a>(j: &'a Json, k: &str) -> Result<&'a Json, String> {
    j.get(k).ok_or_else(|| format!("missing {k}"))
}
fn s(j: &Json) -> Result<&str, String> {
    j.as_str().ok_or_else(|| format!("expected string {j}"))
}
fn a(j: &Json) -> Result<&[Json], String> {
    j.as_arr().ok_or_else(|| format!("expected array {j}"))
}
fn keys(j: &Json, names: &[&str]) -> Result<(), String> {
    let Json::Obj(m) = j else {
        return Err("expected constructor object".into());
    };
    if m.keys().map(String::as_str).collect::<BTreeSet<_>>() != names.iter().copied().collect() {
        return Err(format!("closed constructor keys {j}"));
    }
    Ok(())
}
fn angle(j: &Json, c: &Context) -> Result<u64, String> {
    let v = s(j)?;
    if let Some(b) = c.angles.get(v) {
        return Ok(*b);
    }
    if v.len() != 18
        || !v.starts_with("0x")
        || !v[2..]
            .bytes()
            .all(|b| b.is_ascii_digit() || (b'a'..=b'f').contains(&b))
    {
        return Err("angle needs binary64 hex".into());
    }
    let bits = u64::from_str_radix(&v[2..], 16).map_err(|_| "bad angle")?;
    if !f64::from_bits(bits).is_finite() {
        return Err("nonfinite angle".into());
    }
    Ok(bits)
}
fn value(j: &Json, ty: &Json, c: &Context) -> Result<Json, String> {

        if bound_type != ty {
            return Err("forwarded value type mismatch".into());
        }
        v
    } else {
        j
    };
    fn decimal(j: &Json) -> Result<&str, String> {
        let t = s(j)?;
        if t.is_empty()
            || !t.bytes().all(|b| b.is_ascii_digit())
            || (t.len() > 1 && t.starts_with('0'))
        {
            return Err("noncanonical unsigned decimal".into());
        }
        Ok(t)
    }
    fn fits(t: &str, width: usize) -> bool {

            let mut carry = 0;
            for d in &mut digits {
                let v = carry * 10 + *d;
                *d = v / 2;
                carry = v % 2;
            }
